Second transcription's note: Open: the instructions do not say what to enter for someone who was eligible on December 1 but not for the whole year and does not use the last-month rule. Rule 5 covers only someone not eligible on December 1. Pub. 969's greater-of rule in effect invokes the last-month rule. Chosen: the greater of the worksheet limitation and the December 1 maximum applies when the last-month rule is used (and you were eligible on December 1) or when you were eligible for all 12 months; otherwise the worksheet limitation alone applies. With the last-month rule, the greater-of also covers months in which you actually had coverage (Pub. 969: 'if you didn't otherwise have coverage'). The age-55 increase follows the chart and rule 6: $5,300 for self-only coverage; $9,550 for family coverage only if unmarried.

Ruling: representation (+ ambiguity); project retained. Form 8889 (2025), line 3, and i8889 p. 4, Line 3, rule 3: 'If you were, or were considered, an eligible individual for the entire year and you did not change your type of coverage, enter $4,300 for a self-only HDHP or $8,550 for a family HDHP on line 3' (rule 6 adds $1,000 at 55 or older). The project takes that amount directly when coverage_year is self-only or family all year and the month-based figure when it is changed. triage.py: 1,358 of 1,358 of 1,438 agree; no completed-return difference; the value differences are incomplete or refused records (classm.f8959.2, mutation.t2.mfs_sli_line5, audit.f8889.married_hoh_family_age55 and partials of them: changed coverage with the married family catch-up, the register's line 3 readings, unchanged by this round).

### 4 — Enter the amount you and your employer contributed to your Archer MSAs for 2025 from Form
Status: **AGREE**

### 5 — Subtract line 4 from line 3. If zero or less, enter -0-
Status: **AGREE**

### 6 — Enter the amount from line 5. But if you and your spouse each have separate HSAs and had f
Status: **DISAGREE**

The allocation checks use household.hsa_agreed_count (spouses reporting an agreed allocation) and household.hsa_share_sum (total of the spouses' shares), on the reading that the agreed allocation must be the same for both spouses and must not exceed the limit.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: f8889.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One Form 8889 (counts this HSA beneficiary's form)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.one}
    note: "The IRS pages do not define this interface. Read as a count of 1 per Form 8889 entry, so other forms can count HSA forms (for example, whether both spouses have separate HSAs)."

  - id: f8889.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: f8889.h_self
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-only HDHP maximum contribution ($4,300)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: hsa.self_only}

  - id: f8889.h_self55
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-only HDHP maximum contribution if age 55 or older at the end of 2025 ($5,300)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: hsa.self_only_55}

  - id: f8889.h_fam
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Family HDHP maximum contribution ($8,550)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: hsa.family}

  - id: f8889.h_fam55
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Family HDHP maximum contribution if unmarried and age 55 or older at the end of 2025 ($9,550)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: hsa.family_55}

  - id: f8889.h_self_amt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-only coverage amount from the Line 3 Limitation Chart: $4,300; if you were age 55 or older at the end of 2025, $5,300"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_self
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_self55

  - id: f8889.h_fam_amt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Family coverage amount from the Line 3 Limitation Chart: $8,550; if, at the end of 2025, you were unmarried and age 55 or older, $9,550"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_fam
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_fam
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_fam55
    note: "For a married person with family coverage, the $1,000 additional contribution amount is figured on line 7 and not on line 3."

  - id: f8889.h_months_bad
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: family months more than eligible months, or eligible months more than 12"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [f8889.months_family, f8889.months_eligible]}
        - {op: test, gt: [f8889.months_eligible, {const: 12, type: Count}]}

  - id: f8889.h_ws_self_e
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-only chart amount times number of eligible months"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: f8889.h_self_amt, count: f8889.months_eligible}

  - id: f8889.h_ws_self_f
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-only chart amount times number of family months"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: f8889.h_self_amt, count: f8889.months_family}

  - id: f8889.h_ws_self
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 Limitation Chart total for the months with self-only coverage"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.h_ws_self_e, take: f8889.h_ws_self_f}

  - id: f8889.h_ws_fam
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 Limitation Chart total for the months with family coverage"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: f8889.h_fam_amt, count: f8889.months_family}

  - id: f8889.h_ws_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 Limitation Chart and Worksheet: total for all months"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8889.h_ws_self, f8889.h_ws_fam]}
    note: "A month in which you were enrolled in Medicare or were not an eligible individual is entered as -0-. Such months are excluded from months_eligible."

  - id: f8889.h_ws_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limitation. Divide the total by 12"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.h_ws_total, rate: {param: hsa.twelfth}}

  - id: f8889.h_dec_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Maximum amount that can be contributed based on the type of HDHP coverage you had on the first day of the last month of your tax year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.dec_coverage, {const: "none"}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.dec_coverage, {const: "self_only"}]}
          then: f8889.h_self_amt
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.dec_coverage, {const: "family"}]}
          then: f8889.h_fam_amt

  - id: f8889.h_greater
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The greater of the Line 3 Limitation Chart limitation or the maximum based on December 1 coverage"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [f8889.h_ws_limit, f8889.h_dec_max]}

  - id: f8889.h_greater_applies
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Eligible individual (or considered one under the last-month rule) for the entire year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.last_month, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, ne: [f8889.dec_coverage, {const: "none"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.months_eligible, {const: 12, type: Count}]}

  - id: f8889.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "If you were under age 55 at the end of 2025 and, on the first day of every month during 2025, you were, or were considered, an eligible individual with the same coverage, enter $4,300 ($8,550 for family coverage). All others, see the instructions for the amount to enter"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "self_only"}]}
          then: f8889.h_self_amt
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "family"}]}
          then: f8889.h_fam_amt
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "changed"}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_greater_applies, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_greater
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "changed"}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_greater_applies, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_ws_limit
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported, {when: f8889.h_months_bad, when_known: true}]
    note: "Open: the instructions do not say what to enter for someone who was eligible on December 1 but not for the whole year and does not use the last-month rule. Rule 5 covers only someone not eligible on December 1. Pub. 969's greater-of rule in effect invokes the last-month rule. Chosen: the greater of the worksheet limitation and the December 1 maximum applies when the last-month rule is used (and you were eligible on December 1) or when you were eligible for all 12 months; otherwise the worksheet limitation alone applies. With the last-month rule, the greater-of also covers months in which you actually had coverage (Pub. 969: 'if you didn't otherwise have coverage'). The age-55 increase follows the chart and rule 6: $5,300 for self-only coverage; $9,550 for family coverage only if unmarried."

  - id: f8889.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Enter the amount you and your employer contributed to your Archer MSAs for 2025 from Form 8853, lines 1 and 2. If you or your spouse had family coverage under an HDHP at any time during 2025, also include any amount contributed to your spouse's Archer MSAs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported]
    note: "Archer MSA contributions are screened out by f8889.unsupported, so the supported case is zero."

  - id: f8889.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "Subtract line 4 from line 3. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.line3, take: f8889.line4}

  - id: f8889.h_family_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Had (or treated as having) family HDHP coverage at any time during 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "family"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "changed"}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [f8889.months_family, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "changed"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.last_month, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.dec_coverage, {const: "family"}]}

  - id: f8889.h_family_all
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Treated as having family coverage for each month"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "family"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "changed"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.months_family, {const: 12, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "changed"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.last_month, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.dec_coverage, {const: "family"}]}
    note: "Under the last-month rule with family coverage on December 1, you are treated as having family coverage for the entire year."

  - id: f8889.split
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You and your spouse each have separate HSAs and had family coverage under an HDHP at any time during 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.hsa_both, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_any, {const: true}]}
    note: "Read as the line 6 condition for spouses who share the family limit. It uses household.hsa_both (both spouses on the return have a Form 8889). If either spouse had family coverage, the months_family and dec_coverage questions already count it as family coverage for both spouses."

  - id: f8889.h_fam_months_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 refigured using the worksheet amounts only for the months you were treated as having family coverage (total divided by 12)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.h_ws_fam, rate: {param: hsa.twelfth}}

  - id: f8889.h_step1_refig
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Refigured line 5 (refigured line 3 minus the same line 4)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.h_fam_months_limit, take: f8889.line4}

  - id: f8889.step1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1. Contribution limit to be divided between spouses (line 5 if treated as having family coverage for each month; otherwise line 5 refigured using only the family-coverage months)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_all, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.line5
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_all, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_step1_refig
    note: "Open: Step 1 says to put on line 3 'the total of the worksheet amounts' for the family months. Chosen: that total divided by 12 (the worksheet's limitation), as the worksheet does."

  - id: f8889.h_share_equal
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1 amount divided equally between you and your spouse"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.step1, rate: {param: hsa.half}}

  - id: f8889.share_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Your allocable share of the family contribution limit (Step 1 less the part allocated to your spouse)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.allocation, {const: "equal"}]}
          then: f8889.h_share_equal
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.allocation, {const: "agreed"}]}
          then: f8889.allocated

  - id: f8889.h_self_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4. Other contribution limits for the tax year (worksheet amounts for the months without family coverage, divided by 12)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.h_ws_self, rate: {param: hsa.twelfth}}

  - id: f8889.h_other_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Other contribution limits that apply for the tax year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_all, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_all, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_self_limit

  - id: f8889.h_line6_split
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 result plus Step 4 other contribution limits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8889.share_raw, f8889.h_other_limit]}

  - id: f8889.h_alloc_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: amount allocated to you is more than the Step 1 limit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.allocation, {const: "agreed"}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [f8889.allocated, f8889.step1]}

  - id: f8889.h_alloc_mixed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: only one spouse reports an agreed (unequal) allocation"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.hsa_agreed_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}

  - id: f8889.h_alloc_sum_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: the spouses' agreed allocations together exceed the Step 1 limit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.allocation, {const: "agreed"}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [household.hsa_share_sum, f8889.step1]}

  - id: f8889.line6
    printed: "6"
    label: "Enter the amount from line 5. But if you and your spouse each have separate HSAs and had family coverage under an HDHP at any time during 2025, see the instructions for the amount to enter"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.line5
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_line6_split
    refuses:
      - {when: f8889.h_alloc_over, when_known: true}
      - {when: f8889.h_alloc_mixed, when_known: true}
      - {when: f8889.h_alloc_sum_over, when_known: true}
    note: "Open: Step 4 ('any other contribution limits') is not spelled out. Chosen: the worksheet amounts for the self-only months divided by 12, and zero when you are treated as having family coverage for each month. The allocation checks use household.hsa_agreed_count (spouses reporting an agreed allocation) and household.hsa_share_sum (total of the spouses' shares), on the reading that the agreed allocation must be the same for both spouses and must not exceed the limit."

  - id: f8889.h_add_amt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional contribution amount ($1,000)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: hsa.additional}

  - id: f8889.h_add_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional Contribution Amount Worksheet line 1: $1,000 × number of months eligible"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: f8889.h_add_amt, count: f8889.months_family}

  - id: f8889.h_add_months
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional Contribution Amount Worksheet line 2: divide line 1 by 12"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.h_add_count, rate: {param: hsa.twelfth}}

  - id: f8889.line7
    printed: "7"
    label: "If you were age 55 or older at the end of 2025, married, and you or your spouse had family coverage under an HDHP at any time during 2025, enter your additional contribution amount. See instructions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_any, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_any, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_all, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_add_amt
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_any, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_all, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_add_months
    note: "Open: 'number of months eligible' on the Additional Contribution Amount Worksheet. Chosen: the months meeting items (1) and (2), that is, family-coverage months in which you were eligible. The $1,000 for self-only months is already in the line 3 chart ($5,300)."

  - id: f8889.line8
    printed: "8"
    label: "Add lines 6 and 7"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8889.line6, f8889.line7]}

  - id: f8889.line10
    printed: "10"
    label: "Qualified HSA funding distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported]
    note: "Qualified HSA funding distributions are screened out by f8889.unsupported, so the supported case is zero."

  - id: f8889.line11
    printed: "11"
    label: "Add lines 9 and 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8889.line9, f8889.line10]}

  - id: f8889.line12
    printed: "12"
    label: "Subtract line 11 from line 8. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.line8, take: f8889.line11}

  - id: f8889.h_13_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The smaller of line 2 or line 12"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f8889.line2, f8889.line12]}

  - id: f8889.line13
    printed: "13"
    label: "HSA deduction (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.line2, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.line2
        - guard: {op: test, gt: [f8889.line2, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.h_13_min
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported]
    note: "Enter the smaller of line 2 or line 12. When line 2 is zero, the smaller is zero whatever line 12 is. Prior-year excess contributions (Form 5329 line 43 addition) are screened out by f8889.unsupported."

  - id: f8889.h_contrib_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Your contributions (line 2) plus employer contributions and funding distributions (line 11)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8889.line2, f8889.line11]}

  - id: f8889.excess_amt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Excess HSA contributions for 2025: your excess (line 2 over line 13) plus excess employer contributions (line 9 over line 8)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.h_contrib_total, take: f8889.line8}
    note: "Open: the interface's meaning is not stated. Chosen: the total for Form 5329 line 47, the excess of line 2 over line 12 plus the employer's excess over line 8. With line 10 zero, that equals (line 2 + line 11 − line 8), floored at zero. Withdrawals of excess are screened out by f8889.unsupported."

  - id: f8889.h_rollover_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: amount rolled over is more than total distributions (line 14a)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f8889.rollover, f8889.line14a]}

  - id: f8889.line14b
    printed: "14b"
    label: "Distributions included on line 14a that you rolled over to another HSA. Also include any excess contributions (and the earnings on those excess contributions) included on line 14a that were withdrawn by the due date of your return. See instructions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.line14a, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, gt: [f8889.line14a, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.rollover
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported, {when: f8889.h_rollover_over, when_known: true}]
    note: "Amounts rolled over are part of line 14a, so with no distributions this line is zero. Withdrawn excess contributions are screened out by f8889.unsupported."

  - id: f8889.line14c
    printed: "14c"
    label: "Subtract line 14b from line 14a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.line14a, take: f8889.line14b}
    note: "The line 14b consistency check keeps 14b no more than 14a, so this floor never changes the IRS subtraction."

  - id: f8889.line15
    printed: "15"
    label: "Qualified medical expenses paid using HSA distributions (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.line14a, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, gt: [f8889.line14a, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.medical
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported]
    note: "Only distributions are counted here, so with no distributions this line is zero."

  - id: f8889.line16
    printed: "16"
    label: "Taxable HSA distributions. Subtract line 15 from line 14c. If zero or less, enter -0-. Also, include this amount in the total on Schedule 1 (Form 1040), Part I, line 8f"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.line14c, take: f8889.line15}

  - id: f8889.h_17b_all
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "20% of all distributions included on line 16"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.line16, rate: {param: hsa.additional_rate}}

  - id: f8889.h_17b_part
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "20% of the distributions included on line 16 that do not meet any of the exceptions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.subject_part, rate: {param: hsa.additional_rate}}

  - id: f8889.h_subject_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: part subject to the additional tax is more than line 16"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f8889.subject_part, f8889.line16]}

  - id: f8889.line17b
    printed: "17b"
    label: "Additional 20% tax (see instructions). Enter 20% (0.20) of the distributions included on line 16 that are subject to the additional 20% tax. Also, include this amount in the total on Schedule 2 (Form 1040), Part II, line 17c"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.line16, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, gt: [f8889.line16, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.line17a, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_17b_all
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, gt: [f8889.line16, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.line17a, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_17b_part
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported, {when: f8889.h_subject_over, when_known: true}]
    note: "If no exception applies (line 17a not checked), all of line 16 is subject to the tax. The death exception and mistaken distributions are screened out by f8889.unsupported. The product is rounded half-up to the cent."
```
```
